entryLink id=r74-ba-crohne-cmd-hq-centurion-ret-command-rhino name=Legion Rhino Armoured Carrier target=transport-rhino type=selectionEntry hidden=false text=
  TARGET selectionEntry id=transport-rhino name=Legion Rhino Armoured Carrier type=model text=4 || 11 || 11 || 10 || Transport Capacity: 10 models. Models in Terminator Armour, models equipped with Jump Packs, and models mounted on Bikes or Jetbikes may not embark. Access Points: one on each side and one at the rear. Fire Points: up to two transported models may fire through the top hatch. || If the Rhino is Immobilised, during its controlling player's Shooting phase it may give up firing all weapons and roll a D6. On a 6, remove one Immobilised result; it may move normally from the beginning of its following Movement phase. || The Rhino includes Smoke Launchers and a Searchlight and uses their normal ProHammer rules. || At the start of the controlling player's Movement phase, if the Rhino is Immobilised, roll a D6. On a 4+, remove one Immobilised result and the vehicle may move normally during that Movement phase.
entryLink id=r74-ba-crohne-cmd-hq-centurion-ret-command-drop name=Legion Drop Pod target=transport-drop-pod type=selectionEntry hidden=false text=
  TARGET selectionEntry id=transport-drop-pod name=Legion Drop Pod type=model text=4 || 12 || 12 || 12 || Transport Capacity: 10 models. Models in Terminator Armour count as two models. Models equipped with Jump Packs, Bikes or Jetbikes may not embark. || Uses the normal ProHammer Deep Strike rules except as modified below. || The Drop Pod and its passengers begin in Reserve. At the beginning of the controlling player's first turn, choose half of all friendly vehicles with Drop Pod Assault, rounding up; those Drop Pods arrive automatically using Deep Strike. The remainder use normal Reserves. Passengers must immediately disembark when the Pod lands and may not charge that turn. No unit may embark on the Pod after its original passengers disembark. || If the Drop Pod would scatter onto impassable terrain or another model, reduce the scatter distance by the minimum amount required to avoid the obstacle. If no legal position is possible, resolve the Deep Strike normally. || After arriving, the Drop Pod may not move for the remainder of the battle. It counts as having suffered an Immobilised result but suffers no additional damage for doing so. || 12" || 5 || - || Heavy 1, Large Blast || Friendly units arriving by Deep Strike within 6" of a Locator Beacon do not scatter.
entryLink id=r74-ba-crohne-cmd-hq-centurion-ret-command-dreadclaw name=Legion Dreadclaw Drop Pod target=transport-dreadclaw type=selectionEntry hidden=false text=
  TARGET selectionEntry id=transport-dreadclaw name=Anvillus Pattern Dreadclaw Drop Pod type=model text=4 || 12 || 12 || 12 || Transport Capacity: 10 models in Power Armour, or 5 models in Terminator Armour, or one Dreadnought where specifically permitted. Passengers may disembark from any point around the hull. || Uses the normal ProHammer Deep Strike rules except as modified below. || The Dreadclaw and its passengers begin in Reserve. At the beginning of the controlling player's first turn, roll a D6; on a 4+, it becomes available. From the second turn onward use normal Reserve rules. When available it enters by Deep Strike. Passengers do not immediately disembark. A unit may not charge during the same turn in which the Dreadclaw arrived by Deep Strike. || If the Dreadclaw would scatter onto impassable terrain or another model, reduce the scatter distance by the minimum amount required to avoid the obstacle. || Units disembarking from a Dreadclaw may charge during the same turn in which they disembark. This does not allow a unit to charge during the same turn in which the Dreadclaw arrived by Deep Strike. || After landing, the Dreadclaw remains operational and moves as a Fast Skimmer. It may continue to transport its original passengers. Once those passengers have completely disembarked, the Dreadclaw may not embark another unit.

Multiple Death Masks are not cumulative.

GORECHILD Gorechild is a Master-crafted Power Weapon which grants Khârn +3 Strength. At the beginning of the first round of each close combat, roll a D3. Khârn gains that many additional Attacks for the duration of that round. THE BLOODY Whenever Khârn rolls a natural 1 To Hit in close combat, that attack instead hits the nearest friendly model within 6". If several friendly models are equally close, the opposing player chooses which is hit. Resolve the hit using Khârn's current Strength and the weapon being used. The friendly model may take any saves normally permitted. Wounds caused in this manner do not count towards the close-combat result. If no friendly model is within 6", the roll of 1 is simply a miss. He knows no other psychic powers. Typhon takes the Psychic Test for Aura of Pestilence using Leadership 7 instead of the Leadership value shown in his profile. AURA OF PESTILENCE At the beginning of either player's Assault phase, Typhon may attempt to invoke Aura of Pestilence. If the Psychic Test is passed, every enemy model within 2" of Typhon suffers -1 Attack, to a minimum of 1, until the end of that Assault phase. If the Psychic Test is failed, every friendly model within 2" of Typhon instead suffers -1 Attack, to a minimum of 1, until the end of that Assault phase. Typhon may fight normally during an Assault phase in which Aura of Pestilence is used. If so, the Maniple acts normally until the beginning of the next Thousand Sons Movement phase. If no friendly Thousand Sons Psyker is within 12", the Maniple must take a Leadership test. If the test is failed: • The Maniple may not Advance. • The Maniple may not charge. • If it shoots, it must target the nearest visible eligible enemy unit. GLOBAL CAT rule id=r41-unit-xv-6-ahzek-ahriman-source name=Source Entry target=None type=None text=Ahzek Ahriman | 5 | 5 | 4 | 4 | 3 | 5 | 3 | 10 | 2+/4+ | Force Organisation: | • HQ | Unit Type: | • Infantry (Character) | Wargear: | • Artificer Armour | • Iron Halo | • Black Staff of Ahriman | • Bolt pistol | • Frag grenades | Special Rules: | • Legiones Astartes (Thousand Sons) | • Independent Character | • Master of the Legion | • Psyker (Mastery Level 3\) | • Corvidae | • Chief Librarian | • Ahriman's Cabal | RESTRICTION | Ahriman may only be included in a Thousand Sons army of 1,500 points or more. | OPTIONS | • Ahriman may take: | • Krak grenades .................................. +2 points | CORVIDAE Ahriman always belongs to the Corvidae and may not select another Prosperine Cult. He receives both the Corvidae Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery benefits. CHIEF LIBRARIAN Ahriman knows every psychic power in the ProHammer Divination discipline. He does not randomly select or choose a limited number of Divination powers before the battle. Ahriman still follows the normal ProHammer restrictions governing how many psychic powers a Psyker may successfully invoke during a player turn. BLACK STAFF OF AHRIMAN The Black Staff is a Master-crafted Prosperine Force Weapon. Once during each player turn, Ahriman may re-roll one failed Psychic Test. The second result must be accepted. If the re-rolled Psychic Test also fails, Ahriman suffers Perils of the Warp in addition to any other normal consequences. Activating the Black Staff as a Force Weapon does not count as invoking a psychic power. AHRIMAN'S CABAL Ahriman may select one Legion Command Squad as his personal retinue. The squad does not occupy a separate Force Organisation slot. A Command Squad selected in this manner may purchase Ahriman's Cabal for +50 points. He receives both the Raptora Cult Arcana and Cult Mastery benefits. MAGISTER OF THE RAPTORA Phosis selects both of his psychic powers from the ProHammer Telekinesis discipline. He otherwise follows all normal ProHammer rules for a Mastery Level 2 Psyker. ELDRITCH FORCE FIELD Phosis has a 4+ Invulnerable Save. Against shooting attacks, this is improved to a 3+ Invulnerable Save. While Phosis is joined to a friendly Thousand Sons Infantry unit, models in that unit receive a 5+ Invulnerable Save against both shooting and close-combat attacks. If a model already possesses an Invulnerable Save, improve it by one instead. An Invulnerable Save improved by this rule may never become better than: • 3+ against shooting attacks; • 4+ against close-combat attacks. This rule may combine with the normal Raptora Cult benefits, but the limits above may never be exceeded. COMMAND RETINUE Phosis may select one Legion Command Squad as his retinue. The squad does not occupy a separate Force Organisation slot.

ARMOUR OF SHADES The Armour of Shades grants Amon a 2+ Armour Save. Amon receives a 5+ Cover Save even while in open ground. If Amon would normally receive a Cover Save from terrain or another rule, improve that Cover Save by two steps, to a maximum of 3+. THE HIDDEN ONE While Amon is joined to a friendly Thousand Sons Infantry unit, that unit receives the same protection as his Armour of Shades: • it receives a 5+ Cover Save while in open ground; • any existing Cover Save is improved by two steps, to a maximum of 3+. Amon and any Infantry unit he has joined also gain Infiltrate. This rule may not be conferred to: • models wearing any form of Terminator Armour; • Bikes; • Jetbikes; • Jump Infantry. GLOBAL CAT rule id=r41-unit-xvii-0-covenant-zealot-mob-source name=Source Entry target=None type=None text=Covenant Zealot | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 1 | 3 | 1 | 7 | 6+ | Legion Overseer | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 | 2 | 4 | 2 | 9 | 3+/5+ | SPECIAL RULES DRIVEN TO SLAUGHTER While the Legion Overseer is alive, the Covenant Zealot Mob is Fearless and ignores all Leadership penalties. If the unit is able to declare a charge against an enemy unit during the Assault Phase, it must declare a charge. If more than one enemy unit may be charged, the controlling player may choose which enemy unit is charged. During an Assault Phase in which the unit charges, all Covenant Zealots gain +1 Attack and +1 Initiative for that Assault Phase. The Legion Overseer does not receive these bonuses. If the Legion Overseer is slain, the unit immediately loses Fearless and Driven to Slaughter for the remainder of the battle. If the unit is not locked in close combat when the Overseer is slain, it immediately becomes Pinned. If the unit is locked in close combat, it becomes Pinned as soon as that combat ends, provided it is still on the battlefield. Once the unit recovers from being Pinned, it functions normally and uses its own Leadership for all subsequent tests. Instead, its commanders could be isolated, its communications severed and its strength rendered meaningless before the decisive blow was struck. | In battle the Raven-Lord seemed less a general than an apparition, descending upon vital positions before disappearing again into smoke and darkness. His Legion reflected this philosophy, favouring reconnaissance, sabotage and precisely delivered assaults over wars of attrition. GLOBAL CAT selectionEntry id=r42-role-xiv-0-effects-superior-firepower-legion-veteran-squads-veteran-unit name=Legion Veteran Squad target=None type=unit text=Every model is equipped with Power Armour, a Bolter, Bolt Pistol, Chainsword and Frag Grenades unless replaced by an option below. || All models have the Legiones Astartes special rule and And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic. They are not Fearless. || When constructing the army, select one Veteran Tactic for this squad. The chosen ability applies to every model in the squad for the duration of the battle. || If not equipped with Jump Packs, the squad may select a Rhino, Drop Pod, Dreadclaw Drop Pod or Land Raider where permitted. Land Raider choices will become selectable when their vehicle entries are added; the rule remains in force now. || The Reaping is one of the characteristic attack formations of the XIV Legion. Heavy infantry and fire-support units advance in a relentless column, purging shattered cities and fortified battlefields through weight of fire and remorseless attrition. | EFFECTS | SUPERIOR FIREPOWER | Legion Veteran Squads and Legion Heavy Support Squads may be selected as non-compulsory Troops choices. | They may not fulfil compulsory Troops selections unless another rule specifically permits them to do so. | IMPLACABLE | All Death Guard Infantry units gain the Move Through Cover special rule. | This includes models wearing any form of Terminator Armour. | DARK ARSENAL | Any Death Guard Character or Independent Character may purchase Rad Grenades for +10 points. | Rad Grenades follow the normal rules presented in the Legiones Astartes Army List. | LIMITATIONS | Units in the Detachment may not make Advance Moves. | Vehicles in the Detachment may not move Flat Out. | Units in the Detachment may not deploy using Deep Strike. Units which are required to deploy using Deep Strike may not be selected. | The normal Death Guard restr

Power Armour, Bolter, Bolt pistol and Frag grenades. The unit has Legiones Astartes; the Sergeant is a Character. || The Sergeant may select up to 50 points of additional permitted weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury. Normal Sergeant weapon limits apply. Unit-specific replacements take precedence over duplicate Armoury purchases. || The Reaping is one of the characteristic attack formations of the XIV Legion. Heavy infantry and fire-support units advance in a relentless column, purging shattered cities and fortified battlefields through weight of fire and remorseless attrition. | EFFECTS | SUPERIOR FIREPOWER | Legion Veteran Squads and Legion Heavy Support Squads may be selected as non-compulsory Troops choices. | They may not fulfil compulsory Troops selections unless another rule specifically permits them to do so. | IMPLACABLE | All Death Guard Infantry units gain the Move Through Cover special rule. | This includes models wearing any form of Terminator Armour. | DARK ARSENAL | Any Death Guard Character or Independent Character may purchase Rad Grenades for +10 points. | Rad Grenades follow the normal rules presented in the Legiones Astartes Army List. | LIMITATIONS | Units in the Detachment may not make Advance Moves. | Vehicles in the Detachment may not move Flat Out. | Units in the Detachment may not deploy using Deep Strike. GLOBAL CAT rule id=r46-al-reward-29-r41-unit-xii-1-red-butcher-squad-source name=Source Entry target=None type=None text=Red Butcher | 3 | 2 | 4 | 4 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 9 | 2+/4+ | Force Organisation: | • Elites | RAVENING MADMEN Red Butchers always hit models with a Weapon Skill on a 3+ in close combat, regardless of the normal Weapon Skill comparison. Enemy models likewise always hit Red Butchers on a 3+.
